# Knowledge Bot Setup

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Beta feature
> 
> This is a beta feature. While this feature is currently available for use, you may encounter occasional bugs or stability issues. Contact your customer success manager for more details.

## 

Overview

BotBonnie's Knowledge Bot uses generative AI to automatically respond to users' questions. You can upload materials to train the chatbot with information about your business, such as business hours, return policies, troubleshooting instructions, and product specifications. In the case that the chatbot is unable to answer the question, the Knowledge Bot can transfer the user to human agents for assistance.

Using the Knowledge Bot kit, you can:

  * Leverage generative AI to answer user's questions
  * Add documents and URLs to train the chatbot
  * Transfer users to human agents when needed



* * *

## 

Knowledge bot setup

### 

1\. Add the kit to your flow

In the **Chat > Flows** page, select **Knowledge Bot** , then click **Start editing** to begin editing the kit.

![](https://files.readme.io/6a61d04-Screen_Shot_2023-12-21_at_3.04.10_PM.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/57c8fb3-Screen_Shot_2023-10-03_at_11.32.36_AM.png)

* * *

### 

2\. Add content to train the bot

It is highly recommended to add content and train the chatbot with information specific to your business. The content should include information that will help the chatbot answer questions frequently asked by your users. Here are some examples:

  * Business hours, return policy, shipping options, pricing
  * Troubleshooting instructions, user manuals, product specifications
  * Membership benefits, course curriculum, tour itinerary



Knowledge Bot supports the following types of content. Make sure the documents meet the requirements below.

  * URLs
  * Documents
    * Supported formats are PDF, TEXT, XLSX, and CSV. Text files must be UTF-8 encoded.
    * Encrypted or password-protected documents are not supported.
    * Each file must be less than 5 MB.



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
>   * Knowledge Bot will only train based on the text content. Images won’t be processed.
>   * Knowledge Bot doesn't automatically re-train. If you make changes to the webpage content after the URLs are trained, you will need to click **Start training** again to re-train the bot with the updated content.
>   * The maximum number of documents and URLs that can be uploaded per kit may be different based on the Knowledge Bot plan you are subscribed to.
> 


To add content, click **Add content** and select **URL** or **Document**.

![](https://files.readme.io/19cb33f-Screen_Shot_2023-12-27_at_4.27.52_PM.png)

  * **Document** : Select the files you want to upload.
  * **URL** : Under **Source page URL** , you can enter the URL of a source page and click **Fetch** to retrieve all URLs found on this source page. You can also add URLs one by one under **Specific URLs**. Click **Add** when you are done.

![](https://files.readme.io/8be6d0c-Screen_Shot_2023-12-29_at_3.48.39_PM.png)

If you want to bulk delete or download the contents added, select the items in the list and click **Bulk actions**. Only documents can be downloaded.

![](https://files.readme.io/674e203-Screen_Shot_2023-12-27_at_5.18.06_PM.png)

* * *

### 

3\. Train the bot

After adding the content, click **Start training**. The bot is usually trained within a day. You'll receive an email notification when completed.

![](https://files.readme.io/6f1d139-Screen_Shot_2023-12-27_at_5.20.22_PM.png)

#### 

Bot training status

Please note the following training statuses for the chatbot.

  * When you first add the kit, the status of the chatbot will be **Not trained**.
  * After you've added content and clicked **Start training** , the status will change to **Training**.
  * Finally, when the bot has finished training the content you've added, the status will be changed to **Trained**.

![](https://files.readme.io/a0be07d-knowledge-bot-statuses.png)

#### 

Stopping bot training

If you stop the bot's training before it has completed, e.g. to upload more documents, you'll need to restart the bot training by clicking **Start training** again, then wait for the training to finish. This will restart the training process.

* * *

### 

4\. Set up auto replies for different scenarios

Go to **Reply settings** to set up automatic bot replies to handle different scenarios. BotBonnie provides default auto replies to handle common scenarios such as letting users contact live agents. You can also create your own custom replies.

![](https://files.readme.io/10fba6b-Screen_Shot_2024-02-20_at_5.41.22_PM.png)

#### 

Custom reply

You can create up to 20 custom replies that can be sent to the user when the user's input includes an exact match of at least one of the keywords you entered.

To create a custom reply, click **Add auto reply**. Enter the reply name and the keywords to trigger the reply. Next, set the reply module and switch on the toggle.

  * The keyword matching is not case-sensitive.
  * The user's input needs to contain at least one of the keywords. If there are multiple words in a keyword, the user input needs to include all words in that keyword in the same order.  
Here are some examples:
    * Keywords: `Order` , `PO number`
    * User input: `I would like to check the status of my order.` >> Matched
    * User input: `PO is 12345. Please check status` >> Not matched

![](https://files.readme.io/12b8c4b-Screen_Shot_2024-02-21_at_2.59.55_PM.png)

#### 

Live agent reply

The live agent reply is designed to transition users to live agents when needed. This reply is sent to the user when a system-related issue has occurred or when the user's input exactly matches one of the keywords.

Adjust or add keywords if needed. Next, click the reply module on the right to adjust the default reply message.

  * The keyword matching is not case-sensitive.
  * The user's input needs to equal one of the keywords and cannot include other content.  
Here are some examples:
    * Keyword: `live agent`
    * User input: `live Agent` >> Matched
    * User input: `Agent live` >> Not matched
    * User input: `I need live agent` >> Not matched

![](https://files.readme.io/97c5813-Screen_Shot_2024-02-21_at_3.04.23_PM.png)

#### 

Unable-to-answer reply

This reply is triggered when the chatbot is unable to answer the user’s question. Click the reply module on the right to adjust the default reply message.

![](https://files.readme.io/4c36250-Screen_Shot_2024-02-21_at_3.04.34_PM.png)

* * *

### 

5\. Adjust the flow modules

In addition to the chatbot, the Knowledge Bot includes the following reply modules to handle special scenarios. You can adjust the module messages based on your specific needs.

  * **Start AI chatbot** : You can let the users know that they are speaking with an chatbot. You can also let users know what keywords to type if they want to speak to a human agent.
  * **Unable-to-answer reply** : Inform the users when the bot is unable to answer the question.
  * **Live agent reply** : When a trigger is met, ask the users if they need assistance from a human agent.
  * **Competitor reply** : When the user enters a competitor keyword, let users know that the chatbot cannot answer questions about competitors.
  * **Reach limit response** : When the message limit has been reached, let users know that the chatbot is out of response capacity.
  * **Leave message** : Ask users to leave a message for the human agent.

![](https://files.readme.io/3fbbdc7-Screen_Shot_2024-02-21_at_3.10.17_PM.png)

* * *

### 

6\. Test your bot and create custom answers

Test your Knowledge Bot to see what types of responses it gives. If you aren't satisfied with the responses, you can upload more training materials or create custom answers.

Custom answers are responses you can provide to the bot that are tailored to specific questions for enhanced accuracy and precision. The custom answer will be used to respond to variations of the original question, so even if users asks it in different ways, they'll get the same helpful response.

> 👍
> 
> Test messages don't count towards your message quota.

There are four categories of responses and different operations (uploading training materials, creating custom answers) are available depending on the response type:

Response type| Description| Available operations  
---|---|---  
AI response| Knowledge Bot generated this response.| Create a custom answer for this question.  
Custom answer| Knowledge Bot responded with a custom answer.| Edit the custom answer for this question.  
Fallback response| Knowledge Bot is unable to answer to this question and responded with the Unable-to-answer reply.| • Add content for additional training.  
• Create a custom answer for this question.  
Module auto reply| Knowledge Bot sent a conversation module as a response.| No operations available.  
  
Start testing your Knowledge Bot by entering the **AI chatbot** module and clicking **Test Knowledge Bot** , then enter your question.

![](https://files.readme.io/c0ef860-test-knowledge-bot.png)

After asking a question, you'll see the Knowledge Bot's response. Depending on the response type, you'll be able to add content for additional training or create a custom answer for this question.

![](https://files.readme.io/c51e6c7-add-content-customize-answer.png)

To see the custom answers you've created, go to the **Custom answer** tab.

![](https://files.readme.io/c61a537-custom-answers-tab.png)   


* * *

## 

FAQ

### 

What languages are supported by Knowledge Bot?

Knowledge Bot supports English, Chinese, Japanese, and Korean.

### 

If I have multiple Knowledge Bot kits, will the content added to one kit interfere with another kit?

No, the content added for a Knowledge Bot kit is independent and not used by other Knowledge Bot kits.

### 

How is the message limit calculated?

Each time the Knowledge Bot generates an answer with AI to reply to a user's question, it counts as one message toward the limit. Default replies, such as the live agent reply or competitor reply, do not count toward the limit.

When the message quota runs out, the Knowledge Bot will be disabled until the next billing cycle. During this time, the users will be directed to a live agent. You will receive email notifications when you are approaching the message limit.

To see your current message usage and limit for Knowledge Bot, go to **Chat > Settings > Billing details**.

![](https://files.readme.io/e45968b-Screen_Shot_2023-12-21_at_10.34.53_AM.png)

Under the Knowledge Bot plan, you can find the following information.

  * **Messages used** : The number of messages already generated by AI during this billing cycle.
  * **Message limit** : The total number of messages that can be generated by AI in a billing cycle, including carry-over and add-on limit.
    * **Carry-over** : The unused monthly messages remaining from the previous month.
    * **Add-on limit** : The number of messages that can be used at a charge when the monthly limit and carry-over messages are used up.
  * **Kit added** : The number of Knowledge Bot kits you have added to the flow.
  * **Kit limit** : The total number of Knowledge Bot kits you can add to the flow.



__Updated 12 months ago

* * *

# Knowledge Bot Setup

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Beta feature
> 
> This is a beta feature. While this feature is currently available for use, you may encounter occasional bugs or stability issues. Contact your customer success manager for more details.

## 

Overview

BotBonnie's Knowledge Bot uses generative AI to automatically respond to users' questions. You can upload materials to train the chatbot with information about your business, such as business hours, return policies, troubleshooting instructions, and product specifications. In the case that the chatbot is unable to answer the question, the Knowledge Bot can transfer the user to human agents for assistance.

Using the Knowledge Bot kit, you can:

  * Leverage generative AI to answer user's questions
  * Add documents and URLs to train the chatbot
  * Transfer users to human agents when needed



* * *

## 

Knowledge bot setup

### 

1\. Add the kit to your flow

In the **Chat > Flows** page, select **Knowledge Bot** , then click **Start editing** to begin editing the kit.

![](https://files.readme.io/6a61d04-Screen_Shot_2023-12-21_at_3.04.10_PM.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/57c8fb3-Screen_Shot_2023-10-03_at_11.32.36_AM.png)

* * *

### 

2\. Add content to train the bot

It is highly recommended to add content and train the chatbot with information specific to your business. The content should include information that will help the chatbot answer questions frequently asked by your users. Here are some examples:

  * Business hours, return policy, shipping options, pricing
  * Troubleshooting instructions, user manuals, product specifications
  * Membership benefits, course curriculum, tour itinerary



Knowledge Bot supports the following types of content. Make sure the documents meet the requirements below.

  * URLs
  * Documents
    * Supported formats are PDF, TEXT, XLSX, and CSV. Text files must be UTF-8 encoded.
    * Encrypted or password-protected documents are not supported.
    * Each file must be less than 5 MB.



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
>   * Knowledge Bot will only train based on the text content. Images won’t be processed.
>   * Knowledge Bot doesn't automatically re-train. If you make changes to the webpage content after the URLs are trained, you will need to click **Start training** again to re-train the bot with the updated content.
>   * The maximum number of documents and URLs that can be uploaded per kit may be different based on the Knowledge Bot plan you are subscribed to.
> 


To add content, click **Add content** and select **URL** or **Document**.

![](https://files.readme.io/19cb33f-Screen_Shot_2023-12-27_at_4.27.52_PM.png)

  * **Document** : Select the files you want to upload.
  * **URL** : Under **Source page URL** , you can enter the URL of a source page and click **Fetch** to retrieve all URLs found on this source page. You can also add URLs one by one under **Specific URLs**. Click **Add** when you are done.

![](https://files.readme.io/8be6d0c-Screen_Shot_2023-12-29_at_3.48.39_PM.png)

If you want to bulk delete or download the contents added, select the items in the list and click **Bulk actions**. Only documents can be downloaded.

![](https://files.readme.io/674e203-Screen_Shot_2023-12-27_at_5.18.06_PM.png)

* * *

### 

3\. Train the bot

After adding the content, click **Start training**. The bot is usually trained within a day. You'll receive an email notification when completed.

![](https://files.readme.io/6f1d139-Screen_Shot_2023-12-27_at_5.20.22_PM.png)

#### 

Bot training status

Please note the following training statuses for the chatbot.

  * When you first add the kit, the status of the chatbot will be **Not trained**.
  * After you've added content and clicked **Start training** , the status will change to **Training**.
  * Finally, when the bot has finished training the content you've added, the status will be changed to **Trained**.

![](https://files.readme.io/a0be07d-knowledge-bot-statuses.png)

#### 

Stopping bot training

If you stop the bot's training before it has completed, e.g. to upload more documents, you'll need to restart the bot training by clicking **Start training** again, then wait for the training to finish. This will restart the training process.

* * *

### 

4\. Set up auto replies for different scenarios

Go to **Reply settings** to set up automatic bot replies to handle different scenarios. BotBonnie provides default auto replies to handle common scenarios such as letting users contact live agents. You can also create your own custom replies.

![](https://files.readme.io/10fba6b-Screen_Shot_2024-02-20_at_5.41.22_PM.png)

#### 

Custom reply

You can create up to 20 custom replies that can be sent to the user when the user's input includes an exact match of at least one of the keywords you entered.

To create a custom reply, click **Add auto reply**. Enter the reply name and the keywords to trigger the reply. Next, set the reply module and switch on the toggle.

  * The keyword matching is not case-sensitive.
  * The user's input needs to contain at least one of the keywords. If there are multiple words in a keyword, the user input needs to include all words in that keyword in the same order.  
Here are some examples:
    * Keywords: `Order` , `PO number`
    * User input: `I would like to check the status of my order.` >> Matched
    * User input: `PO is 12345. Please check status` >> Not matched

![](https://files.readme.io/12b8c4b-Screen_Shot_2024-02-21_at_2.59.55_PM.png)

#### 

Live agent reply

The live agent reply is designed to transition users to live agents when needed. This reply is sent to the user when a system-related issue has occurred or when the user's input exactly matches one of the keywords.

Adjust or add keywords if needed. Next, click the reply module on the right to adjust the default reply message.

  * The keyword matching is not case-sensitive.
  * The user's input needs to equal one of the keywords and cannot include other content.  
Here are some examples:
    * Keyword: `live agent`
    * User input: `live Agent` >> Matched
    * User input: `Agent live` >> Not matched
    * User input: `I need live agent` >> Not matched

![](https://files.readme.io/97c5813-Screen_Shot_2024-02-21_at_3.04.23_PM.png)

#### 

Unable-to-answer reply

This reply is triggered when the chatbot is unable to answer the user’s question. Click the reply module on the right to adjust the default reply message.

![](https://files.readme.io/4c36250-Screen_Shot_2024-02-21_at_3.04.34_PM.png)

* * *

### 

5\. Adjust the flow modules

In addition to the chatbot, the Knowledge Bot includes the following reply modules to handle special scenarios. You can adjust the module messages based on your specific needs.

  * **Start AI chatbot** : You can let the users know that they are speaking with an chatbot. You can also let users know what keywords to type if they want to speak to a human agent.
  * **Unable-to-answer reply** : Inform the users when the bot is unable to answer the question.
  * **Live agent reply** : When a trigger is met, ask the users if they need assistance from a human agent.
  * **Competitor reply** : When the user enters a competitor keyword, let users know that the chatbot cannot answer questions about competitors.
  * **Reach limit response** : When the message limit has been reached, let users know that the chatbot is out of response capacity.
  * **Leave message** : Ask users to leave a message for the human agent.

![](https://files.readme.io/3fbbdc7-Screen_Shot_2024-02-21_at_3.10.17_PM.png)

* * *

### 

6\. Test your bot and create custom answers

Test your Knowledge Bot to see what types of responses it gives. If you aren't satisfied with the responses, you can upload more training materials or create custom answers.

Custom answers are responses you can provide to the bot that are tailored to specific questions for enhanced accuracy and precision. The custom answer will be used to respond to variations of the original question, so even if users asks it in different ways, they'll get the same helpful response.

> 👍
> 
> Test messages don't count towards your message quota.

There are four categories of responses and different operations (uploading training materials, creating custom answers) are available depending on the response type:

Response type| Description| Available operations  
---|---|---  
AI response| Knowledge Bot generated this response.| Create a custom answer for this question.  
Custom answer| Knowledge Bot responded with a custom answer.| Edit the custom answer for this question.  
Fallback response| Knowledge Bot is unable to answer to this question and responded with the Unable-to-answer reply.| • Add content for additional training.  
• Create a custom answer for this question.  
Module auto reply| Knowledge Bot sent a conversation module as a response.| No operations available.  
  
Start testing your Knowledge Bot by entering the **AI chatbot** module and clicking **Test Knowledge Bot** , then enter your question.

![](https://files.readme.io/c0ef860-test-knowledge-bot.png)

After asking a question, you'll see the Knowledge Bot's response. Depending on the response type, you'll be able to add content for additional training or create a custom answer for this question.

![](https://files.readme.io/c51e6c7-add-content-customize-answer.png)

To see the custom answers you've created, go to the **Custom answer** tab.

![](https://files.readme.io/c61a537-custom-answers-tab.png)   


* * *

## 

FAQ

### 

What languages are supported by Knowledge Bot?

Knowledge Bot supports English, Chinese, Japanese, and Korean.

### 

If I have multiple Knowledge Bot kits, will the content added to one kit interfere with another kit?

No, the content added for a Knowledge Bot kit is independent and not used by other Knowledge Bot kits.

### 

How is the message limit calculated?

Each time the Knowledge Bot generates an answer with AI to reply to a user's question, it counts as one message toward the limit. Default replies, such as the live agent reply or competitor reply, do not count toward the limit.

When the message quota runs out, the Knowledge Bot will be disabled until the next billing cycle. During this time, the users will be directed to a live agent. You will receive email notifications when you are approaching the message limit.

To see your current message usage and limit for Knowledge Bot, go to **Chat > Settings > Billing details**.

![](https://files.readme.io/e45968b-Screen_Shot_2023-12-21_at_10.34.53_AM.png)

Under the Knowledge Bot plan, you can find the following information.

  * **Messages used** : The number of messages already generated by AI during this billing cycle.
  * **Message limit** : The total number of messages that can be generated by AI in a billing cycle, including carry-over and add-on limit.
    * **Carry-over** : The unused monthly messages remaining from the previous month.
    * **Add-on limit** : The number of messages that can be used at a charge when the monthly limit and carry-over messages are used up.
  * **Kit added** : The number of Knowledge Bot kits you have added to the flow.
  * **Kit limit** : The total number of Knowledge Bot kits you can add to the flow.

# LINE Beacon

LINE Beacon enables your bot to detect when users enter a beacon region and interact with them using personalized messages. By integrating [LINE Beacon](https://developers.line.biz/en/docs/partner-docs/line-beacon/) with BotBonnie, you can deliver timely and relevant communications tailored to your business needs.

For example, you can use LINE Beacon in:

  * Retail stores: Greet customers with a welcome message, share promotions, provide store navigation assistance upon entry.
  * Events or exhibitions: Facilitate faster check-ins, deliver schedules or materials, guide participants to specific locations.



### 

Prerequisites

Before you begin, you must have:

  * [Connect your LINE Official Account](doc:channels-line) to BotBonnie.
  * A LINE Beacon service configured for API integration.
  * Received your LINE Beacon hardware IDs from the service application.



For hardware specifications and setup instructions, see the [LINE Beacon documentation](https://developers.line.biz/en/docs/messaging-api/using-beacons/).

* * *

## 

Add a scenario

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Important
> 
>   * Sending LINE Beacon notifications is limited to once per hour per user to ensure a better user experience and reduce message fatigue.
>   * LINE platform messaging fees will apply to messages sent from LINE Beacon.
> 


Follow these steps to create a LINE Beacon scenario in the BotBonnie console:

  1. Navigate to **Content** > **LINE Beacon** , then click **Add scenario**.

![](https://files.readme.io/4f161467de03ea19a9228797ff034ca9879563a177df988fbf332f3aace165c9-line-beacon-add-scenario.png)

  2. Configure the following settings:



Setting| Description  
---|---  
**Scenario**|  Required. Enter a recognizable title for the scenario.  
**Activation**|  Toggle the scenario on or off.  
**Triggered module**|  Required. Select a [module](doc:modules-and-messages#modules) to define the trigger when a device is near the LINE Beacon devices.  
**Triggered actions**|  Add up to five actions to take place when the scenario is triggered. To learn more, see the [Triggered actions](doc:triggered-actions).  
**Beacon ID**|  Required. Add unique identifiers for your beacon devices. Each beacon ID can only be used in one scenario.   
• Manual entry: Enter IDs in the input field, separated by commas, semicolons, or spaces.   
• Bulk upload: Click **Import LINE Beacon ID by CSV** to upload IDs.  
  
![](https://files.readme.io/3a71088ac6c67d0ae52998e23e4f507fb2f1a714d838498ab52a45503916fde7-image_15.png)

  3. Click **Save** to create the scenario.



* * *

## 

View and manage status

Navigate to **Content** > **LINE Beacon** to view all scenarios. The page displays the following details for each scenario:

Column| Description  
---|---  
**Scenario**|  Click the title to view the scenario. Use the icons next to the title to duplicate or delete it.  
**Status**|  Toggle the scenario to activate or deactivate it.  
**Number of IDs**|  Displays the number of Beacon IDs associated with the scenario.  
**Module**|  Displays the triggered module applied to the scenario.  
**Actions**|  Displays the number of triggered actions linked to the scenario.  
  
![](https://files.readme.io/7533cb9d2207c5ab2e98936e8661c0e14d65487c931e3c0261496b33461d1ee8-Group_1.png)

# LINE Smart Link

Use LINE smart links to execute a triggered action (e.g. updating parameters, tags, and demographic attributes) for a user when they click the URL. LINE smart links can be embedded, just like a standard URL, and can also be shared via QR code. After a user clicks a LINE smart link:

  1. The user will be briefly redirected to your LINE Official Account in the LINE app.
  2. Immediately after opening their conversation with your LINE Official Account, they will be automatically redirected to the destination URL you specify.



You can also place a LINE smart link outside the Official Account, e.g. a standard web page. When users who are friends of the LINE Official Account click on the link, their clicks will also be recorded.

### 

Prerequisites

Before creating a LINE smart link, you'll need to create a LIFF app on the LINE Developers Console under the same provider as your Official Account. Please note the following LIFF app requirements:

  * The LIFF app's **Size** setting must be set to **Compact**.
  * **Endpoint URL** must be set to: `https://rd.botbonnie.com/liff/<LINE_OA_ID>`, where `<LINE_OA_ID>` is the ID of the linked Official Account.
  * The following **Scopes** must be selected: **profile** , **openID**.



* * *

## 

Create a LINE smart link

  1. Go to **Growth tools** , click **\+ Add growth tool** , then select **LINE > LINE Smart Link**.

![](https://files.readme.io/867d4c4-nav-add-growth-tool.png)

  2. Enter a name for the LINE smart link campaign.
  3. Under **Operation period** , set the validity period of the link.
     1. **Permanent** : The link will immediately be active and will continue to function until it is deleted.
     2. **Limited-time** : The link is only valid during the specified time period.

![](https://files.readme.io/a87c8f4-line-smart-link-operation-period.png)

  4. Under **Redirect URL** , enter the final destination URL. Note that users who click on the LINE smart link will be briefly redirected to your LINE Official Account before being automatically redirected to the final destination URL.
  5. (Optional) Under **Triggered actions** , add the [triggered actions](doc:triggered-actions) you'd like to execute when the user clicks on the link. For example, you can update the user's parameters or tags.
  6. Under **Channel** , select the connected LINE Official Channel that should be opened when the smart link is clicked.

![](https://files.readme.io/2770157-line-smart-link-channel.png)

  7. Under **LIFF ID Setting - Compact** , the ID of your LIFF app will automatically be populated. If you encounter any issues, please contact your customer success manager for assistance.

![](https://files.readme.io/00b5242-line-smart-link-liff-id.png)

  8. After completing all the required settings, click **Generate** , then copy the short URL or download the QR code to use the LINE smart link.

![](https://files.readme.io/027bf69-line-smart-link-generate.png)

* * *

## 

Performance report

After publishing the smart link, you can open its performance report by clicking the eye icon in the growth tool list. The report includes:

  * The total number of unique users who clicked a URL or scanned a QR code.
  * The total number of URL clicks and QR code scans.
  * Line charts showing the trends of clicks and scans.



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> The report includes clicks and QR scans from both new and existing friends.

![](https://files.readme.io/3dabf23-smart-link-report-performance.png)

* * *

## 

LINE smart link FAQs

### 

If I have already created the LINE smart link and made it public, can I still edit it?

Yes, you can update the URL without changing the channel, and it won't affect URLs and QR codes that have already been published.

### 

Why are some clicks not recorded?

When users click on a link, they can still be redirected to the redirect page even if they don’t become friends of the official account or grant permission for the LINE LIFF app to access their personal information. However, BotBonnie can't tag them or record the click count under these circumstances.

![](https://files.readme.io/3d4597f-image5.png)

# Live Chat

Your chatbot's live chat menu allows you to view the chatbot's ongoing conversations from all channels in a single, unified view. Monitor real-time interactions and provide support when users need help from a live agent.

The live chat page consists of three panels for easy access to all the chat-related information, allowing you to:

  1. Conversation list: Sort, filter, and search all ongoing conversations. When you select a conversation, the other two panels populate with its details.
  2. Chatroom: Send messages, manage the conversation, and use the AI assistant for reply suggestions.
  3. User details: View and edit user attributes, tags, and parameters. In addition, you can add notes about the user for more personalized customer service.

![](https://files.readme.io/18e27e1e50b41632f641702a79262be76bf6e8aacf40d4a48515e4080e12f6d9-live-chat-overview.png)

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Getting started with live chat
> 
>   * Mobile access: Log in to `https://console.appier.com` on your mobile browser.
>   * LINE messaging fees: Messages sent in the **Main chatroom** count toward your LINE quota and may incur fees. Use the live agent chatroom to avoid charges.
>   * Conversation retention: Conversations are stored for six months only.
>   * Auto-pause: The chatbot automatically pauses for 30 minutes when an agent sends a message.
> 


* * *

## 

Limitations

### 

Unsupported message types

Some message types won't appear in your live chat interface due to platform API limitations. Users will still see that their message was sent successfully on their end.

Channel| Unsupported message types  
---|---  
LINE| Contacts  
Instagram| GIF  
WhatsApp| Contact, avatar  
  
### 

User quoted reply visibility limitations

When users quote and reply to messages, you might not see the full conversation context in live chat.

Channel| Supported reply types| Quoted message visibility| Notes  
---|---|---|---  
LINE| All supported types in the messaging channel| Always visible| • Sticker replies appear as text `(emoji)`.  
• Quoted carousel or image carousel messages display only their alt text.  
Messenger, Instagram, WhatsApp| Text only| Not visible when the user replies with a GIF, image, or audio file| The reply appears as a regular message without the quoted context.   
  
For example: Quoted reply visibility with GIF message in Messenger   
  
![](https://files.readme.io/1537ee24ef27b5e2d5716ab6b5a70880a5c17326a2204681fa18cf5ffe36c164-Live-chat-messenger-not-supported-1.png)  
  
> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Tip
> 
> If a user mentions content you can't see, ask them to describe it in a text message or resend it in a supported format.

* * *

## 

Searching and filtering conversations

### 

Search conversations

To search conversations, enter a user's name, tag, phone number, or text from an internal note in the search bar above the message list.

![](https://files.readme.io/47c63e39c51c8c6a555c075dffea72b83e6a7585545b896987b9b8371d9ac61c-live-chat-search.png)

### 

Filter conversations

To filter conversations, use the dropdown menus at the top:

  * **Folder** : **Main** , **Done** , **Unread** , **Spam** , and **Follow Up**. You can change a conversation's category at any time.
  * **Platform** : All available platforms.
  * **Agent** : Assigned customer service agent.

![](https://files.readme.io/541a2fe3f08103febcee71147aa109319967ddb56081aa2f1bd5cd42f4c4dfc1-live-chat-filter.png)

* * *

## 

Managing conversations

When you select a conversation, you can:

  * Send messages
  * Use the AI assistant for reply suggestions
  * Quote and reply to a message
  * Assign conversations to agents or groups
  * Set the conversation's category
  * Pause and resume the chatbot



In addition, the chat menu consists of two chatrooms which you can freely toggle between **Main chatroom** and **Live agent chatroom**.

| Main chatroom| Live agent chatroom  
---|---|---  
Channel| All connected channels| LINE only  
Messaging fees| Conversations on the LINE platform may incur messaging fees| No LINE messaging fees  
Interface| Native channel interface| Web view in LIFF app  
Setup required| Available by default| Requires enabling and button configuration  
  
![](https://files.readme.io/1c31e2e-chatrooms.png)

### 

Send messages in a chatroom

Select a user in the conversation list to open their conversation. You can send:

  * **Text messages** : Enter text directly in the chatroom, or click **AI assistant** for reply suggestions.
  * **Media and files** : Click the attachment icons to add emojis, files, images, videos, or audio.
  * **Flow modules** : Click the message bubble icon and select an existing module from the dropdown. Note that only individual modules are available; modules grouped under a kit can't be selected.
  * Quote and reply: Click the reply icon next to a message to quote and reply to a message from a previous conversation.

![](https://files.readme.io/f1bae70fbbe896d815ff88687dc91c5442be35b5e9c5256d4f281cd00e1f6299-chat-room.png)

### 

Use the AI assistant for reply suggestions

The AI assistant helps agents generate reply suggestions based on the conversation context. You can:

  * Select a suggested response and send it directly.
  * Edit the response before sending.
  * Enter a prompt to adjust the response before generating suggestions.
  * Adjust the AI assistant settings for tone, length, and language.



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Limitations
> 
> Each click on the **AI assistant** generates a new response based on the conversation context. The AI assistant can generate up to 120 responses per bot per day. Contact your customer success manager for details.

To access the AI assistant:

  1. Click the **AI assistant** or **Check suggestion** in the message input area.

![](https://files.readme.io/8673159d4021c62ea4c171a1067e72ad4bbe328be11ce80c79742e514d07ff18-ai-assistant-live-chat.png)

  2. Review the generated response. If needed, you can click one of the following:
     * **Enter prompt** to provide input or instructions to generate a new response.
     * **Edit response** to modify the AI-generated response before sending.



Then, click **Send**.

![](https://files.readme.io/586064246604aa08e21742675cab3bd49ded57714e455fe047c0363f92b4efc7-enter-edit.png)

To customize AI assistant suggestions, click the settings icon next to the AI assistant, adjust the following settings to match your brand voice or customer expectations, and click **Apply** to save changes:

  * **Tone** : Define a tone for your reply:
    * **Professional**
    * **Friendly**
    * **Casual**
    * **Empathetic**
    * **Confident**
  * **Length** : Define your reply length:
    * **Short**
    * **Long**.
  * **Language** : Select a preferred language:
    * English
    * Traditional Chinese
    * Japanese
    * Korean

![](https://files.readme.io/c6c7570b6b81eba1a887c4b7bb226877ba42ea66527fec443f31e550756ddaca-ai-assistant-settings.png)

### 

Quote and reply to a message

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported channels
> 
>   * LINE
>   * WhatsApp
> 


You can quote a specific message from a conversation to provide context for your reply. The availability of this feature and the supported [message types](doc:flows-message-types) vary by channel.

Channel| Message types you can quote| Your reply options (from live chat)  
---|---|---  
LINE| • Text  
• Image  
• Sticker  
• Video  
• Carousel  
• Image carousel| • Text  
• Emoji  
WhatsApp| • Text  
• Image  
• Sticker  
• GIF  
• Video  
• Audio| • Text  
• Emoji  
  
> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> You can't quote the following message types:
> 
>   * Message types not supported in live chat
>   * LINE: Audio, imagemap, and video imagemap messages
> 


To quote and reply to a user's message:

  1. Hover over the message you want to quote. A reply icon will appear.
  2. Click the reply icon. The original message appears quoted in the message field. Enter your reply, then click **Send**.

![](https://files.readme.io/f75e08f985a905455e9542778835d3d37eb6b268852b1f67af9a29be4e835d42-quote-and-reply.png)

### 

Assign conversations to agents or groups

You can assign conversations to agents or agent groups individually or in bulk. Your available assignment options depend on your [role permissions](doc:settings-team-members#customer-services).

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Tip
> 
> Create [live agent groups](doc:settings-live-agent-groups) to automatically route incoming conversations based on predefined [assignment rules](doc:settings-live-agent-groups#assignment-rules), reducing the need for manual assignment.

#### 

Assign a single conversation

  1. Select a conversation from the conversation list.
  2. In the upper-left corner of the chatroom, select an agent or agent group from the dropdown menu.

![](https://files.readme.io/a990aaba6f139699414470a2c213001976ff6b56cf9054436713b7f0916e5daa-assign-agent-group.png)

#### 

Assign multiple conversations

To assign several conversations at once:

  1. Select the conversations in the conversation list:

     * Hover over a conversation's profile picture and select the checkbox next to each conversation.
     * Select the checkbox at the top to choose up to 50 conversations at once.

![](https://files.readme.io/ee9e73ea0ec96b0bfcbc566a9aa9c52e65191497ed7fb070afd74d7913cf66d6-click-to-select-assign.gif)

  2. In the action bar at the top, select **Assign agent**.
  3. Select an agent or agent group from the dropdown, then click **Assign**.

![](https://files.readme.io/031e49608112c4bc31c67055a8822a787071b7616153b562c186941ff265dcf2-assign-agent-selected-users.png) ![](\[assignment modal screenshot\])

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Bulk assignment works with your active filters. Apply filters first to assign a specific group or an agent.

### 

Set a conversation's category

You can organize conversations into categories to streamline your workflow and quickly filter the conversation list. To move a conversation, select a status icon at the top of the chatroom.

Category| Purpose| Action  
---|---|---  
**Main**|  All conversations are moved to this category by default when a user triggers the **Assign to live agent** action. In addition, the chatbot will be paused for 30 minutes by default.| To modify the duration, go to **Settings** > [**Pause bot (Assign to live agent)**](doc:settings-notify-agent#pause-bot-assign-to-live-agent).  
**Done**|  For conversations that are fully resolved. Marking a conversation as **Done** will re-enable the chatbot.| Click the check icon to move a conversation to the **Done** category.  
  
![](https://files.readme.io/4f64418-move-to-done.png)  
  
  
**Follow Up**|  Mark conversations that require a future action or track conversations that are not yet fully resolved.| Click the star icon to mark a conversation as **Follow Up**.  
![](https://files.readme.io/f92f3ea-follow-up.png)  
**Unread** & **Spam**|  Holds new user messages or manually marks conversations as **Unread** or moves them to **Spam**.| Select the three horizontal dots, then select **Mark as unread** or **Mark as spam**.   
  
![](https://files.readme.io/e75c157-mark-unread-spam.png)  
  
  
  
After categorizing conversations, agents can use the folder filter in the conversation list to focus on a specific group of conversations, such as those marked for **Follow Up**.

### 

Pause and resume the chatbot

When handling a conversation, you may need to temporarily disable the chatbot to prevent it from auto-replying while you're interacting with the user.

To manually pause the bot, click the pause bot icon at the top right of the chatroom. Then, select the desired duration to pause the bot. You'll see a banner at the top confirming the bot is deactivated until the timer expires.

![](https://files.readme.io/64f5b831535865a08dc282a2e9ba6fb25c70b2e69265c588a0214def582f7ab8-deactivat-bot.png)

To resume auto-replies immediately, click **Activate bot** on the banner, or click the pause bot icon again.

![](https://files.readme.io/b2a9f11a5c8a08baf6c388ffd63520ac0bf308bbad24a0db9f64edb7d440a417-reactivate-bot.png)

* * *

## 

Managing user details

The user details panel displays profile information right next to your active conversations. Use this panel to:

  * Manage tags and parameters
  * Update profile attributes
  * Manage internal notes
  * View purchase history
  * View user history

![](https://files.readme.io/c55126a10e60b552f2f9d2fc46978e0c2710b5dae769fd702e21cc559c5a7c9b-manage-user-details.png)

### 

View a unified user profile

When a user contacts you through multiple channels with matching identifiers (such as a phone number), you'll see a unified profile in this panel.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Notes
> 
> Conversations remain separate by channel in the conversation list. Only user profile data is unified. To learn more about how profiles are linked, see [User Unification](doc:live-chat-user-unification).

To view a unified profile, select a conversation to open the user details panel. The following sections display consolidated information:

Section| Description  
---|---  
Basic information| Displays the user's avatar, name, and contact details such as **Mobile** and **Email**. Click **View More** to reveal additional fields like **Birthday** , **Location** , and **Gender**.   
  
Note: These details reflect the unified profile determined by the [User unification logic](doc:live-chat-user-unification#user-unification-logic).  
**Linked accounts**|  Shows all channels where this user has contacted you. Hover over a card to view the display name and channel user ID. Select a card to switch the conversation view to that specific channel.  
**[Tags](doc:user-properties#tags)**|  Shows tags aggregated from all linked channels, along with a total count and last updated time. You can add or remove tags.  
**[Parameters](doc:user-properties#parameters)**|  Shows channel-specific parameters that include a channel icon to indicate which channel the data belongs to.  
**Notes**|  Shows internal notes from all linked channels with the source icon, date, and agent name. You can add, edit, or delete the notes directly.  
**Purchase history**|  Shows the user's recent orders from your connected e-commerce platform. To learn more about the supported platforms and retention periods, see Purchase history.  
**User history**|  Shows the user's interaction history across all linked channels. Each record displays the source channel.  
  
> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Understanding data scope
> 
> Parameters are channel-level data specific to each channel. All other profile information is user-level data that appears across all linked channels.

### 

View purchase history

The **Purchase history** tab displays the user's recent orders from your connected e-commerce platform without leaving the chat.

Depending on your platform, this tab shows transactions from the last 14 days (for 91APP) or 90 days (for Shopify, Cyberbiz, and SHOPLINE). For orders older than this limit, use the platform icon to access the user's full history on your e-commerce platform. To display orders in live chat, set up the [E-commerce integration](doc:e-commerce-integrations).

![](https://files.readme.io/4eedd06db5f39486708fa04d52d4be0ad5ef36915b3612d8bda560e8cd0bfc46-purchase-history.png)

### 

View user history

The **User history** tab displays a unified timeline of all user interactions and activities across every linked channel. Use this timeline to understand the user's full journey and activities over time.

![](https://files.readme.io/61d8743468ee5aa12a71d48476ec40f1131acc6e509cc393889fa015dc3eb6e6-user-history.png)

The timeline categorizes events using color-coded labels. Common types include:

  * **Data** : When the user profile is updated, such as adding tags, updating attributes (like phone number or email), or changing parameters.
  * **Assign** : When a conversation is assigned to, unassigned from, or transferred between agents or groups.
  * **EC** (E-Commerce): When an event is synced from your [connected platform](doc:e-commerce-integrations), such as an order placed, account binding, or product tag update.



If the user has linked profiles, this timeline combines events from all connected channels. Use the channel icon next to each event to identify where the activity took place.

![](https://files.readme.io/a2f61cf5be55879fa448ce8bac04fe8a5d8f463679e70db5061aa91493c70893-user-history-channel.png)

* * *

## 

Live agent chatroom

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported channel
> 
>   * LINE
> 


The **Live agent chatroom** allows users to interact seamlessly with your live customer service agents in the LIFF web view within the LINE app.

Once the user enters the web view chatroom, the customer service agent can access the conversation by selecting the **Live agent chatroom** tab. This ensures that messages are being sent within the designated chatroom without any additional LINE messaging fees.

![](https://files.readme.io/26e1eba8248aea83c305a62e518c1e1d14ad89891a78c5bee37ec9d8f826a881-users-chatroom.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/786a0a175f59a4186ed86e52f6136d10b8bfa4d8b01dcd72b6f49183fab52f2b-live-agent-chatroom.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> The **Live agent chatroom** tab doesn't appear by default in the live chat interface, even after configuration. It only becomes visible when a user enters the flow and triggers the chatroom. Once triggered, both the end user and the agent can communicate through this feature.

### 

Set up your chatroom

Follow the steps below to start a live agent chatroom:

  1. Enable the live agent chatroom feature for your LINE Official Account. For details, see [Enabling live agent chatroom](doc:channels-line#enable-live-agent-chatroom-for-line).
  2. Create entry points by adding buttons with the **Open live agent chatroom** on click action. To learn how to configure this option, see [Buttons](doc:button).



### 

Set up unread messages reminders

To enable or disable unread message reminders for the **Live agent chatroom** , click the clock icon at the top of the chatroom.

  * When enabled, the system sends a reminder through LINE if the user hasn’t responded. Additional charges may apply.
  * Customer service agents can disable the reminder anytime if a response from the user is not required.

![](https://files.readme.io/32c5e2c238f14670d94543d8c6635e915a74353642d9aa128bd134043b28c25e-reminder-enabled.png)

You can also configure reminders in [Enabling live agent chatroom for LINE](doc:channels-line#enable-live-agent-chatroom-for-line).

# User Unification

User unification links separate profiles across multiple channels into a single unified profile. This enables your agents to have a more complete and consistent understanding of each user.

When profiles are linked, you'll see consolidated basic user information, tags, notes, purchase history, and user history in the [Live Chat](doc:live-chat) interface. However, the live chat conversations remain separate by channel, and parameters don't sync across profiles.

* * *

## 

How profile linking works

Profiles are automatically linked by comparing identifiers found across channels. The system follows the ID hierarchy to prioritize which identifiers determine a match.

### 

Default ID hierarchy

Identifiers are checked in the following order, from highest to lowest priority:

  1. `user_id (crm_id)`: Highest priority
  2. `ec_crm_id` (e-commerce CRM ID)
  3. `phone_no` (Phone number)
  4. `email` (Email)
  5. `line_uid` (LINE UID)
  6. `messenger_uid` (Messenger ID)
  7. `instagram_uid` (Instagram ID)
  8. `kakao_uid` (Kakao ID)
  9. `whatsapp_uid` (WhatsApp ID)
  10. `viber_uid` (Viber ID)
  11. `zalo_uid` (Zalo ID)
  12. `webchat_uid` (WebChat ID): Lowest priority



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> This is the default ID hierarchy for BotBonnie accounts. If you have questions about your current configuration or need to make changes, contact your customer success manager.

### 

User unification logic

Identifiers are evaluated top-down by priority. The highest-priority matching identifier determines whether profiles are merged.

Data| Logic| Result  
---|---|---  
Match found| The value of the identifier is identical in both profiles.| Profiles are linked. They are merged into a unified profile.  
Different values| The identifier exists in both profiles, but the values differ.| No profiles are linked, even if values for the lower-priority identifiers match.  
Missing value| No value is found in one or both profiles.| The system proceeds to the next identifier in the list to look for a match.  
  
* * *

## 

Triggering profile linking

Profile linking occurs automatically when identifiers are detected.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Notes
> 
>   * No manual action is required to trigger linking.
>   * Once profiles are linked, they can't be unlinked.
> 


Profile linking occurs in two primary scenarios:

  * Manual updates by agents
  * Data provided by users



### 

Manual updates by agents

When an agent adds or edits a high-priority identifier in the [user details panel](doc:live-chat#managing-user-details), profiles are automatically checked for matches. Common identifiers include a phone number or an email.

For example, you're chatting with a user on Instagram and add their phone number `0912345678` to the profile. If a separate LINE profile already exists with that same phone number, and there's no conflict with a higher-priority identifier, the system links them as a unified profile.

### 

Data provided by users

Profiles are also linked automatically when users provide identifiers through automation flows set up in your chatbot:

  * [Survey kit](doc:survey-kit#store-survey-answers-as-user-attributes): When a user submits a survey answer configured to be saved as a user attribute (such as a phone number or email).
  * [Channel linking on WebChat](doc:channels-webchat#step-4-messaging-platform-linking): When a user connects a social account, such as Messenger or LINE, while using WebChat.



For example, a user interacts with your LINE chatbot and submits their email address through a survey kit form. If that email matches an existing Instagram profile, both profiles are linked automatically.

  


__Updated about 2 months ago

# Lucky Wheel

Using the lucky wheel kit, you can create a campaign that invites the users to spin a wheel to get a chance to win a prize. With the lucky wheel kit, you can easily:

  * Customize the appearance of the lucky wheel pages
  * Set the prizes and the probability of winning each prize
  * Export the list of participants to a CSV file
  * View performance report



* * *

## 

Lucky wheel kit setup

### 

1\. Add the kit to your flow

From the left menu, go to **Flow** , then click **Advanced kits** and select **Lucky wheel**.

![ ](https://files.readme.io/486fa4a-nav-lucky-wheel.png)

Click **Start editing** or double-click the kit to begin editing.

![ ](https://files.readme.io/ffb96fd-lucky-wheel-start-editing.png)

### 

2\. Configure basic kit settings

Enter a **Kit name** to help you identify the kit in the Flows, and set a **Campaign period**.

![ ](https://files.readme.io/5645867-lucky-wheel-name-campaign-period.png)

#### 

Number of entries

This is the maximum number of times a user can play the lucky wheel.

  * **Maximum entries per user** : Specify the total number of time users can play the lucky wheel.
  * **Maximum entries per day** : Specify the number of time users can play the lucky wheel each day. The count resets at midnight each day.
  * **Use a parameter value as the number of entries per person** : Dynamically apply different numbers of entries to users based on the parameter value associated with each user.

![ ](https://files.readme.io/716e509-lucky-wheel-number-of-entries.png)

Here's an example of how to use a parameter value as the number of entries. Let's say you want to use this kit to encourage users to get a free trial of a new product in your physical stores by rewarding participants with a chance to play the lucky wheel.

To set this up, go to **Growth tools > Start chat with QR code** and set **Triggered actions > Calculate parameter value** to add `1` to a parameter (e.g. `luckywheel_spin_num`) when the user scans the QR code. After participating in the in-store free trial, users can scan a QR code to get one chance to play the lucky wheel.

![](https://files.readme.io/cf6db63-Screen_Shot_2023-11-07_at_3.40.53_PM.png)

### 

3\. Customize the appearance

Under the Appearance section, you can select a **Theme** and type up to 2 lines of text for the **Headline text**. Next to **Preview** , you can use the drop-down menu on the right to preview what each page looks like.

![](https://files.readme.io/b3cbf6e-Screen_Shot_2024-02-27_at_7.00.06_PM.png)

#### 

Customize appearance

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Enterprise
> 
> The **Customize appearance** option is available for Enterprise Plan only.

**Customize appearance** allows you to change the colors of the visual components and upload your own images to replace the background images, wheel outer frame, and the spin button. Refer to the image specifications shown on the BotBonnie console to prepare the images.

![](https://files.readme.io/56a890d-Screen_Shot_2024-02-27_at_7.01.09_PM.png)

### 

4\. Set up prizes

You can add up to 7 different prizes and set the options for not winning a prize.

#### 

Display "Try next time" in the lucky wheel

Enable this option if:

  * You want the probability of winning a remaining prize to remain unchanged after any prize has all been given out.
  * You don't want the probability of winning a prize to be 100%. Users who didn't win a prize will land on the "Try Next Time" section in the wheel.
  * You want the probably of winning a prize to be 100%, but you still want to display the "Try next time" message after a user spins.



If one of the prizes runs out first, the probability of getting each remaining prize stays the same, and the probability of not winning a prize increases.

![ ](https://files.readme.io/e6edf3f-Screen_Shot_2024-02-27_at_7.44.42_PM.png)

If you want all participants to win a prize, disable the **Display "Try next time" in the lucky wheel** option and make sure the probability of all prizes adds up to 100%. In this scenario, it's recommended to have a high or unlimited quantity of prizes to prevent prizes from running out.

When a prize runs out, the probability of winning the remaining prizes will increase to allow all participants to win a prize. See the When a prize runs out section for details.

![](https://files.readme.io/321639f-Disalbe_didnt_win_a_prize.png)

#### 

Add a prize

To add a prize, click **Add prize**.

![](https://files.readme.io/44bee37-Screen_Shot_2024-02-27_at_7.03.58_PM.png)

For each prize, configure the following settings.

  * **Name** : Give a name to the prize. This name will be displayed to users when they win the prize.
  * **Image** : Upload a 1:1 image less than 1 MB to be the prize image.
  * **Format** : Select a prize format. The following prize formats are supported in the Lucky Wheel kit.



Prize format| Description  
---|---  
**Inform users of prizes won**|  Users are immediately notified of their winning status. This prize type is ideal for digital prizes that don't require a physical exchange to occur.  
  
For example, this prize type can be used for digital assets such as images.  
  
![](https://files.readme.io/0894be5-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_11.52.57_AM.png)  
**Physical exchange**|  This prize type is suitable for brick-and-mortar locations and physical events, where winning users can physically redeem a prize by presenting the winning screen to an employee.  
  
![](https://files.readme.io/a1d9b32-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_12.02.12_PM.png)  
**Unique serial number**|  Upload a CSV file containing a list of unique serial numbers (text, barcode, or URL) that users can exchange for a prize. If this prize type is selected, winners will see the serial number when they win. For example:  
  
• **Text** : Users who win this prize will be assigned a unique serial number from the CSV file in sequential order. Serial number are suitable for coupons in e-commerce shops or rewards in mobile games.  
• **Barcode** : The serial number will be converted and displayed as a barcode. Barcodes are suitable for physical stores with POS machines.  
• **URL** : The CSV file should contain a list of URLs. When users click the button, they will be directed to the URL to receive the reward. URLs are suitable for distributing LINE points, as it allows users to redeem points with a single click.  
  
![](https://files.readme.io/08193c1-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_1.59.22_PM.png)  
  
Please note the following serial number file requirements:  
• Only CSV files are supported  
• The file must be less than 10 MB  
• The file must contain a single column with the list of serial numbers  
![](https://files.readme.io/8b60599-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_2.03.18_PM.png)  
**Fixed serial number**|  Specify a serial number (text, barcode, or URL) that users can exchange for a prize. If this prize type is selected, winners will see the serial number when they win. For example:  
  
• **Text** : Users who win this prize will be shown the serial number. Serial number are suitable for coupons in e-commerce shops or rewards in mobile games.  
• **Barcode** : The serial number will be converted and displayed as a barcode. Barcodes are suitable for physical stores with POS machines.  
• **URL** : When users click the button, they will be directed to the URL to receive the reward. URLs are suitable for distributing LINE points, as it allows users to redeem points with a single click.  
**Point card**|  Select an existing point card kit to send to users who win.  
  
  * **Quantity** : Set the number of prizes to distribute. This setting is unavailable for **Unique serial number** since the number of prizes available will be based on the number of serial numbers in the CSV file.

  * **Probability** : Set the probability of winning this prize. The probability of all prizes can be less than or equal to 100%. The remaining probability will be the chance of not winning a prize. Let's say you have two prizes each with a 10% chance of winning. This means that for each spin, the user has a 20% chance of winning a prize, and an 80% chance of not winning a prize.

  * **Daily maximum** : Set a daily maximum for the number of prizes distributed per day. You can also enable **Divide evenly by days** if you want to distribute approximately the same number of prizes each day for the duration of the campaign. For example, if the quantity of "Prize A" is 40 and the campaign is 10 days, checking this option will distribute "Prize A" up to 4 times per day.


![ ](https://files.readme.io/439c8db-lucky-wheel-daily-maximum.png)

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Tip
> 
> For more details on how BotBonnie calculates the prize distribution and probability, see [Understanding prize distribution and probability](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/lucky-wheel-kit#understanding-prize-distribution-and-probability).

  * **Send a message through chat** : Select this option if you want to send a message to the user who won this prize. You can edit the content of the message in the flows.



### 

5\. Customize the result pages

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Enterprise
> 
> The **Use customized background image** option is available for Enterprise Plan only.

#### 

Prize quantity display

Select if you want to display the prize quantity to users.

  * **Show total quantity** : Let users see the total number of prizes in the prize list.
  * **Show remaining quantity** : Let users see the number of prizes left in the prize list.
  * **Do not show** : Do not show the prize quantity in the prize list.

![](https://files.readme.io/9dcdd25-Screen_Shot_2024-02-27_at_7.06.10_PM.png)

#### 

When a prize is won

Under this section, you can select **Use customized background image** if you want to upload your own background image when a prize is won. This image will replace the default animation for the prize redemption page and the result page when a prize is won. Select **Show prize image** to display the prize image on these two pages on top of the customized background image.

![Screen Shot 2022-10-03 at 2.27.45 PM.png ](https://files.readme.io/55347c4-Screen_Shot_2024-02-27_at_7.16.12_PM.png)

#### 

When no prize is won

This section is available when you have set a probability of not winning a prize.

Under this section, you can select **Use customized background image** if you want to upload your own background image when no prize is won. This image will replace the default animation for the result page when no prize is won.

Select **Send a message through chat** if you want to send a message to the user who didn't win a prize. You can edit the content of the message in the flows.

### 

6\. Add terms and conditions

Modify the terms and conditions of this campaign or type any information about the game to suit your campaign. These terms and conditions are displayed at the bottom of the lucky wheel homepage.

![Screen Shot 2022-10-03 at 2.30.18 PM.png ](https://files.readme.io/4088dd4-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_2.30.18_PM.png)

### 

7\. Other settings

Under normal circumstances, no action is needed in this section. BotBonnie will automatically detect if your channels are successfully connected with BotBonnie. If you run into issues here, contact Appier Support or your customer success manager for assistance.

![](https://files.readme.io/4299fd5-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_12.33.41_AM.png)

### 

8\. Adjust the Flows modules

Go back to the Flows page, and adjust the content of each module to suit your campaign.

![](https://files.readme.io/7e14e2d-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_5.32.34_PM.png)

* * *

## 

Reset kit data

Resetting a kit is useful if you ran the campaign to do internal testing, and now want to clear all test data before officially launching the campaign.

Resetting a kit will clear all data associated with the kit.

  * All records of participant data (e.g. user details, spins, and wins) will be deleted
  * All prizes will be returned to the prize pool
  * The kit report will be cleared



> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Caution
> 
> This action can't be reversed.

In the lucky wheel kit settings, go to the **Reset** section, then click **Reset**. You'll be prompted to type in `Reset` into the input box, then click **Reset** again to confirm.

![](https://files.readme.io/82b1f00-Screen_Shot_2024-03-04_at_7.47.27_PM.png)

* * *

## 

Performance report

To view the performance report of the lucky wheel kit, in the sub flow of the lucky wheel kit, click **Kit Report**.

The report includes prize inventory status, number of participants, and number of spins.

### 

Export participant list

To export the list of participants and their related data, click **Export participant list** in the top-right corner.

![Screen Shot 2022-10-03 at 2.55.18 PM.png ](https://files.readme.io/586846b-Screen_Shot_2024-03-04_at_7.00.46_PM.png)

Type the email address where you'd like the CSV file to be sent to, then click **Confirm**. A CSV file will be sent to you within an hour.

![Screen Shot 2022-10-03 at 3.18.22 PM.png ](https://files.readme.io/e6e6410-Screen_Shot_2024-03-04_at_7.43.04_PM.png)

The exported file contains a list of users who participated in the game with details such as user name, prize won, drawn time...etc.

![](https://files.readme.io/c86f98d-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_3.25.54_PM.png)

### 

Report summary

  * **Total participants** : This is the number of users who played the game, including users who didn't win a prize.
  * **Number of entries** : This is the number of times users played the game, including the draws where no prize is won.
  * **Prize status** : You can monitor the number of prizes left in the prize pool in the **Remaining** column.

![Screen Shot 2022-10-03 at 3.07.34 PM.png ](https://files.readme.io/4f21137-Screen_Shot_2024-03-04_at_7.13.01_PM.png)

### 

Detailed data

Select a time period to see the performance data during that period. There are three line graphs available:

  * Participants
  * Number of prizes won in each category
  * Number of entries

![Screen Shot 2022-10-03 at 3.15.10 PM.png ](https://files.readme.io/8eec3a1-Screen_Shot_2024-03-04_at_7.15.12_PM.png)

* * *

## 

Understanding prize distribution and probability

This section explains how BotBonnie distributes prizes and adjusts the probability of winning a prize under different scenarios.

### 

When the number of prizes per day is not a whole number

If you select **Evenly distribute this prize** in prize settings, BotBonnie tries to distribute the same number of prizes each day during the campaign period.

If the divided number is not a whole number, BotBonnie will **round up** to the next whole number.

For example, let's say you have 10 prize A and the campaign period is 20 days, which equals to 0.5 prizes per day. When distributing prizes, BotBonnie will **round up** this number and distribute up to 1 prize on the first day.

Prize| Quantity| Campaign duration| First day| First 2 days| First 3 days  
---|---|---|---|---|---  
Prize A| 10| 20 days| 0.5 (**Round up to 1**)| 1| 1.5 (**Round up to 2**)  
  
> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> In actual distribution, it's also possible that no prize is drawn on the first day or first two days (e.g. due to too few participants), and 2 prizes are distributed on the third day.

### 

When a prize runs out

When a prize runs out, the distribution of the remaining prizes differs based on whether the **Display "Try next time" in the lucky wheel** option is enabled under the **Didn 't win a prize** section.

If the total probability of winning a prize adds up to 100%:

  * If **Display "Try next time" in the lucky wheel** is enabled, the probability of winning each remaining prize stays the same when a prize runs out.
  * If **Display "Try next time" in the lucky wheel** isn't enabled, after a prize is finished being distributed, the probability of winning of remaining prizes increases to ensure the total probability of winning a prize adds up to 100%.

![](https://files.readme.io/ca2bc6e-Didnt_win_a_prize.png)

For example, let's say you have 2 different prizes, Prize A with a 10% probability and Prize B with a 30% probability. When Prize A runs out, the probability of Prize B stays the same, and the probability of not getting a prize increases.

Prizes| Original probability| Probability after Prize A runs out  
---|---|---  
Prize A| 10%| Runs out  
Prize B| 30%| 30% (Unchanged)  
No prize| 60%| 70%  
  
* * *

If **Display "Try next time" in the lucky wheel** is disabled, BotBonnie will automatically adjust the probability of winning the remaining prizes to allow all participants to win a prize.

![](https://files.readme.io/d5e64f8-Disalbe_didnt_win_a_prize.png)

  * **One remaining prize** : Let's say you have 2 different prizes, Prize A with a 10% probability and Prize B with a 90% probability. When Prize A runs out, the probability of Prize B now becomes 100% to allow all participants to win a prize.



Prizes| Original probability| Probability after Prize A runs out  
---|---|---  
Prize A| 10%| Runs out  
Prize B| 90%| 100%  
No prize| 0%| 0%  
  
  * **Multiple remaining prizes** : If there are multiple remaining prizes, BotBonnie will adjust the probability proportionally. Let's say you have 3 different prizes. Prize A has a 10% probability, Prize B has a 30% probability, and Prize C has a 60% probability. When Prize A runs out, BotBonnie will distribute the 10% chance of winning Prize A proportionally to Prize B and Prize C based on the ratio of Prize B to Prize C (3:6). As a result, the probability of Prize B and Prize C becomes 33.3% and 67.7% respectively.



Prizes| Original probability| Probability after Prize A runs out  
---|---|---  
Prize A| 10%| Runs out  
Prize B| 30%| 33.3%  
Prize C| 60%| 66.7%  
No prize| 0%| 0%  
  
__Updated 6 months ago

* * *

# Menus

Enhance your chatbot's user experience with BotBonnie's advanced menu creation tools. Design and deploy [Messenger persistent menus](https://developers.facebook.com/docs/messenger-platform/send-messages/persistent-menu) and [LINE rich menus](https://developers.line.biz/en/docs/messaging-api/rich-menus-overview) directly from the BotBonnie console to improve your bot's user experience by providing:

  * **Interactive elements** : Incorporate interactive buttons that lead to different chatbot responses, URLs, or other actions within the messaging app.
  * **Streamlined navigation** : Create always-accessible menus that offer users quick access to your chatbot's key features and content.
  * **Personalized functionality** : Create customized menus that can be automatically assigned to different users based on certain conditions. For example, you can display a member-exclusive menu group to users who have successfully linked their social account with your brand account, providing access to features such as order tracking and viewing account details.



### 

Menu types

There are two types of menus you can create.

  * **Default menu group** : All users see this menu by default.
  * **User-level menu groups** : Use a [triggered action](doc:triggered-actions) in your flow to assign a user to a specific menu group. Users who aren't assigned to a user-level menu group will see the default menu group instead.



* * *

## 

Create a rich menu

The required settings differ for different messaging platforms. For setup instructions, refer to the sections below:

  * Messenger menu
  * LINE menu



### 

Messenger menu setup

  1. Go to **Content** > **Menus**.

![](https://files.readme.io/2a1feca359a410f4e0ee7164d18f43b140243fb4743b4beb5453c9713f73dd04-menus-messenger.png)

  2. Click the tab for **Messenger menu** , then under the section for the menu type you'd like to create (either **Default menu groups** or **User-level menu groups**), and click **\+ Add menu**.

![](https://files.readme.io/839398a-add-messenger-menu.png)

  3. Complete the following settings:
     1. **Menu name** : This is only visible from the BotBonnie console.
     2. **Menu type** : Choose whether this menu should always be available (**Persistent menu**) or only available for a specified period of time (**Limited-time menu**).
     3. **Operation Menu Period** : If you set the menu type to **Limited-time menu** , specify the start and end dates and times. A menu timeline will be displayed under this setting, allowing you view the schedules of all your menus.

![](https://files.readme.io/86701f2-messenger-settings-1.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/a7468d4-messenger-operation-menu-period.png)

  4. Under **Persistent Menu Item Settings** , click **\+ Add an item** , then configure a [button](doc:button) or [triggered actions](doc:triggered-actions).

![](https://files.readme.io/c671807-messenger-persistent-menu-item-settings.png)

  5. After you've completed all the settings, go to the top of the page and click **Deploy**.

![](https://files.readme.io/c8a45b0-messenger-menu-deploy.png)

### 

LINE menu setup

  1. Go to **Content > Menus**.

![](https://files.readme.io/9ec76788d3f4f81335db56cac6528bb95944a9b2f74c76d9521bb75207aa59e0-menus-line.png)

  2. Click the tab for **LINE menu** , then under the section for the menu type you'd like to create (either **Default menu groups** or **User-level menu groups**), and click **\+ Add menu**.

![](https://files.readme.io/a927188-add-line-menu.png)

  3. Enter a menu name, choose a default display mode, and choose a menu type.
     1. **Menu name** : This is only visible from the BotBonnie console.
     2. **Default display mode** : Choose whether the menu should be displayed or hidden by default.
     3. **Menu type** : Choose whether this menu should be always be available (**Persistent menu**) or only available for a specified period of time (**Limited-time menu**).

![](https://files.readme.io/31ab0c8-line-menu-settings-1.png)

  4. If you set the menu type to **Limited-time menu** , specify the start and end dates and times under **Operation menu period**. A menu timeline will be displayed under this setting, allowing you to view the schedules of all your menus.

![](https://files.readme.io/8b4b914-operation-menu-period.png)

  5. Under **Image upload and layout settings** , choose the menu layout size. Next, you'll be able to upload a base menu image, select a menu layout, and configure the desired menu interactions.

![](https://files.readme.io/d997b1e-line-menu-image.png)

  6. After you've completed all the settings, click **Deploy** at the bottom of the page.

![](https://files.readme.io/46584be-line-menu-deploy.png)

#### 

Menu layout size

The LINE menu offers two layout sizes for different design needs. Select a layout that best fits your images and improves user interaction.

![](https://files.readme.io/0a0bb06ddc78e46d068107e01e8918763751e5161f13c7103bbc624df0be878a-menu-size.png)

Layout size| Compact| Large  
---|---|---  
Description| Optimized for simpler menu designs.| A larger layout for more detailed visuals.  
Menu tabs| Supports only a single menu.| Supports up to four menu tabs.  
Supported dimensions| • 2500 × 843 • 1200 × 405 • 800 × 270| • 2500 × 1686 • 1200 × 810 • 800 × 540  
File formats| • JPG • JPEG • PNG| • JPG • JPEG • PNG  
File size| Up to 1 MB.| Up to 1 MB.  
  
> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> When using the **Large** layout with multiple tabs, account for the height of the menu tabs by including padding at the top of your base images. This ensures a sufficient tappable area, allowing users to switch tabs smoothly.
> 
> The required padding for menu tabs in each layout size is as follow:
> 
>   * 2500 × 1686 px: 200 px
>   * 1200 × 810 px: 96 px
>   * 800 × 540 px: 64 px
> 

> 
> The example below shows the 1200 × 810 px layout. Leave enough space at the top so users can switch between tabs.
> 
> ![](https://files.readme.io/55bd2cd57172621480a56d742b600a58322e2917d20130ce92305aba9e9507f9-large-menu-tabs.v2.png)
> 
> See the [layout size reference](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1HxjAQ6Dzg3uFQuTX1xkbxnJ3_N0Hnd7l/view) for details on other layout sizes.

* * *

## 

View menu groups

Go to **Content > Menus** to view all of your menu groups. The menus page displays the following details for each menu group:

  * **Menu name** : The name you set for the menu group.
  * **Operational status** : The current status of the menu group, indicating whether users can see the menu group or not.
  * **Operation period** : The duration which the menu group is valid for. If the menu group isn't on a limited schedule, this column will display **Permanent**.
  * **Last edited** : The last time the menu group's settings were edited.

![](https://files.readme.io/15a1f6fb8955e82e60396a254facf2c9d305aa78168ab3877202cb6ce31168b0-menus-title.png)

To see a schedule of all your menu groups, click the three dots next to the menu group type, then click **View menu timeline**.

![](https://files.readme.io/e77001d-menu-timeline.png)
